j44asF(c%2Cm)%7Bvar wU12h_n%3Dfunction()%7Breturn c(...arguments)%7D%3Breturn Object.defineProperty(wU12h_n%2C"length"%2C%7Bvalue%3Am%2Cconfigurable%3A!0%7D)%2CwU12h_n%7D%7D)()%3B'

def _remote_to_py(value: Any) -> Any:
    """Normalize nodriver RemoteObject ke plain Python."""
    deep_serialized = getattr(value, 'deep_serialized_value', None)
    if deep_serialized is not None:
        deep_value = getattr(deep_serialized, 'value', None)
        if deep_value is not None:
            return _remote_to_py(deep_value)
    direct_value = getattr(value, 'value', None)
    if direct_value is not None and value.__class__.__name__ in {
        'RemoteObject',
        'DeepSerializedValue',
    }:
        return _remote_to_py(direct_value)
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        keys = set(value.keys())
        if 'value' in value and keys.issubset(
            {'type', 'value', 'description', 'subtype', 'className'}
        ):
            return _remote_to_py(value.get('value'))
        return {str(k): _remote_to_py(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, list):
        if all(
            isinstance(item, (list, tuple))
            and len(item) == 2
            and isinstance(item[0], str)
            for item in value
        ):
            return {item[0]: _remote_to_py(item[1]) for item in value}
        return [_remote_to_py(item) for item in value]
    return value


async def _inject_js(tab) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Inject JS ke tab via CDP — pola identik add_friend.py."""
    # JS tetap URL-encoded, decode via eval(decodeURIComponent()) di browser
    inj = await tab.send(
        uc.cdp.runtime.evaluate(
            expression=(
                '(() => { try {'
                f' eval(decodeURIComponent({json.dumps(_SETTING_PROFILE_JS)}));'
                ' return JSON.stringify({ok:true}); }'
                ' catch(e) { return JSON.stringify({ok:false, error:String(e)}); } })()'
            ),
            return_by_value=True,
        )
    )
    ro = inj[0] if isinstance(inj, tuple) else inj
    s = getattr(ro, 'value', None)
    try:
        return json.loads(s) if s else {'ok': False, 'error': 'empty_result'}
    except Exception:
        return {'ok': bool(s), 'raw': str(s)[:200]}


async def run(
    tab,
    account,
    cfg,
    *,
    project_root: Path = Path('.'),
    on_progress=None,
    cancel_event=None,
    on_session_expired=None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Run setting_profile feature.

    Inject bookmarklet JS ke tab Facebook yang sudah login,
    tunggu hingga semua mutations selesai.
    """
    sp_cfg = cfg.get('setting_profile') or {}
    wait_seconds = int(sp_cfg.get('wait_seconds', 15))

    def _result(success, status, stage, error='', diagnostic=None):
        return {
            'success': success,
            'status': status,
            'stage': stage,
            'sukses': 1 if success else 0,
            'gagal': 0 if success else 1,
            'total': 1,
            'error': error,
            'diagnostic': diagnostic or {},
        }

    if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
        return _result(False, 'cancelled', 'cancelled', 'cancelled')

    from core import fb_client
    await fb_client.install_helpers(tab)
    tokens = await wait_user_loaded(tab, account.uid, max_wait=15)
    if tokens is None:
        recovery = await recover_gate_then_wait_user(
            tab, account, max_wait=10, on_session_expired=on_session_expired
        )
        tokens = recovery.get('tokens')
    if tokens is None:
        return _result(False, 'error', 'verify_login', 'page_not_loaded setelah 15s')

    user_id_str = str(tokens.get('userId') or '')
    if user_id_str != str(account.uid):
        recovery = await recover_gate_then_wait_user(
            tab, account, max_wait=10, on_session_expired=on_session_expired
        )
        if recovery.get('tokens'):
            tokens = recovery['tokens']
            user_id_str = str(tokens.get('userId') or '')
    if user_id_str != str(account.uid):
        return _result(
            False, 'suspicious', 'verify_login',
            f'suspicious userId={user_id_str} != {account.uid}',
            {'expected_uid': account.uid, 'actual_uid': user_id_str},
        )

    if on_progress:
        on_progress({'phase': 'setting_profile', 'done': 0, 'total': 1})

    if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
        return _result(False, 'cancelled', 'cancelled', 'cancelled')

    log.info('[%s] setting_profile: injecting JS, wait=%ds', account.uid, wait_seconds)

    try:
        inj_result = await _inject_js(tab)
    except Exception as exc:
        log.warning('[%s] setting_profile inject error: %s', account.uid, exc)
        inj_result = {'ok': False, 'error': str(exc)}

    if not inj_result.get('ok'):
        if on_session_expired is not None:
            recovery = await recover_session_for_retry(
                tab, account,
                on_session_expired=on_session_expired,
                log_prefix=f'[{account.uid}] setting_profile',
            )
            if recovery.get('recovered'):
                log.info('[%s] setting_profile: retry setelah relogin', account.uid)
                try:
                    inj_result = await _inject_js(tab)
                except Exception as exc2:
                    inj_result = {'ok': False, 'error': str(exc2)}

        if not inj_result.get('ok'):
            return _result(False, 'error', 'inject', inj_result.get('error', 'inject_failed'), inj_result)

    log.info('[%s] setting_profile: inject ok, waiting %ds...', account.uid, wait_seconds)
    await tab.wait(wait_seconds)

    log.info('[%s] setting_profile: selesai', account.uid)

    if on_progress:
        on_progress({'phase': 'setting_profile', 'done': 1, 'total': 1, 'sukses': 1, 'gagal': 0})

    return _result(
        True, 'ok', 'done',
        diagnostic={'inject_result': inj_result, 'wait_seconds
